campaign/setting/notable-figures/force-grey/01-vajra-safahr-the-blackstaff.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Trollskull Alley**, **Fireball!**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, **Kolat Towers**, **Vault of Dragons**, **Consulting Hlam**, **The Dragon in the Harbor**, **The Trouble with Meloon**, **Destroy the Intellect Factory**, **The Legate's Eyes**, **Smoke in the Tower**, **The Sleeping Asset**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/harpers/01-mirt.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Trollskull Alley**, **Fireball!**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, **Kolat Towers**, **Vault of Dragons**, **The Talking Mare**, **The Dead Drop**, **The Doppelganger Auditions**, **A Friend's House**, **The Sleeping Asset**, **The Stone's Other Master**, **The Doppelganger Problem**, **The Dreamer's Reach**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/harpers/05-corene-wyldath.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Corene Wyldath is a reliable Harper informant embedded in Xanathar's Dock Ward operation for four months under the cover name Halla Ironstave, sending good intelligence. To those who pay attention, something shifted three weeks ago; her reports are still accurate, but she is asking different questions. She carries Nihiloor's Occupying Devourer, a custom variant that keeps her mind intact, so she can be freed if the devourer is forced out. Deep down, Corene is still in there, aware and unable to act. The devourer is careful not to leave gaps large enough to trigger an alert.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/harpers/05-corene-wyldath.md:25: **Relationships.** Corene is a Harper deep-cover agent embedded in the Dock Ward operation of Xanathar's Guild, the subject of Harper mission five (**The Sleeping Asset**), and the most dangerous kind of asset: one who cannot confirm her own loyalty.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-adversaries/04-filthy-meg.md:25: **Relationships.** Filthy Meg is an independent merfolk information broker operating out of Mistshore — she is not on Xanathar's payroll and not a BD asset, which is precisely what makes her valuable. She has existing relationships with both Jarlaxle (who gave her a ring of true sight as a retainer payment and gets harbor intelligence in return) and the Cassalanters (who have paid for Field Ward surveillance twice). Her network of homeless residents in the Field Ward is her primary collection mechanism: they notice who passes through, where people sleep, and what disappears. She resurfaces in **Sea Maidens Faire** as the best independent source on the Sea Maidens Faire's crew movements and in **Vault of Dragons** on Brandath Crypts foot traffic.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/02-renaer-neverember.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Trollskull Alley**, **Fireball!**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Vault of Dragons**, **The Stone's Other Master**, **The Dragon in the Harbor**, **Gralhund Villa (location)**, **Zhentarim Warehouse**, **Xanathar Sewer Hideout**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/04-floon-blagmaar.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Trollskull Alley**, **Zhentarim Warehouse**, **Xanathar Sewer Hideout**, **Trollskull Manor**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/05-meloon-wardragon.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Force Grey (occupied by a Xanathar's Guild intellect devourer after the Field of Triumph, about three tendays before the first day of the watch)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/05-meloon-wardragon.md:16: **Motivations.** The real Meloon wants to fight alongside people he admires, honor Tymora through brave action, and protect Waterdeep. The intellect devourer wants to steer adventurers away from Undermountain, serve Xanathar's interests, and avoid detection.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/05-meloon-wardragon.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Meloon Wardragon is a barrel-chested warrior who enters every room at full volume — laugh, handshake, Azuredge at his hip and demonstrated on request. He fills taverns with warmth and expects nothing back for it. To those he trusts, he is the friend who shows up unprompted, who rescued Vajra alongside Renaer without being asked and never mentioned it again, who carries quiet grief for colleagues he has outlived. Deep down, Nihiloor's intellect devourer has marked him as a target precisely because he is trusted, well-connected, and constitutionally unable to believe that a friend's body could be used against him. The devourer occupies him after the Field of Triumph, about three tendays before the first day of the watch, and it keeps his brain alive and reads it. It has taken him into the lair twice by then. The wrongness is harder to name than to describe: the third person, the implausible Azuredge excuses, the drinks he never used to order, the directions through the sewers that lead straight into Xanathar patrols.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/05-meloon-wardragon.md:24: **Morale.** Before the occupation, Meloon fights with reckless generosity, placing himself between allies and the worst of it and trusting Azuredge's return-throw completely. While the intellect devourer rides him, he fights competently as a Warrior Veteran but steers outcomes to serve Xanathar's interests. He never draws Azuredge and never swears. Each dawn the devourer must succeed on a DC 15 Charisma save to touch the axe, and the axe is Meloon's anchor when the party forces the devourer out. Once it is out, the real Meloon wakes with 1 level of Exhaustion and speaks only in fragments until a Long Rest. Then he remembers the whole occupation as a long dream, and he never knows what the devourer passed on.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/06-dalakhar.md:15: **Motivations.** Dalakhar wanted to deliver the Stone of Golorr to the party (planning to reclaim it later for Neverember), avoid the Zhentarim and Xanathar agents closing in, and serve the man he believed was Waterdeep's rightful Open Lord.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/06-dalakhar.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Dalakhar was a small gnome with dry waste on his boots and sewer-stink on his cloak. To those he trusted, he was Neverember's most reliable agent, the one who succeeded where many others had failed. Deep down, he was afraid that the Zhentarim and Xanathar's Guild were close to catching him, and he was right.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/independents-allies/19-aurinax.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Fireball!**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Kolat Towers**, **Vault of Dragons**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/lords-alliance/01-jalester-silvermane.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Trollskull Alley**, **Fireball!**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, **Kolat Towers**, **Vault of Dragons**, **Lords' Alliance First Meeting**, **The Long Watch**, **The Exile's Return**, **The Red Wizard's Price**, **The Archer Above**, **Fentree's Ledger**, **An Audience with the Open Lord**, **Suspension and Dismissal**, **Escalation to the Open Lord**, **Redknife**, **Stingblade**, **Warduke**, **Lioncrown**, **The Sleeping Asset**, **The Stone's Other Master**, **The Doppelganger Problem**, **Force Grey Mission 5 — The Legate's Eyes**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/manshoons-zhentarim/01-manshoon.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Trollskull Alley**, **Fireball!**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, **Kolat Towers**, **Vault of Dragons**; **The Talking Mare**, **The Dead Drop**, **The Doppelganger Auditions**, **A Friend's House**, **The Stone's Other Master**, **The Poisoned Delivery**, **Silencing Skeemo**, **The Yellowspire Job**, **Ziraj's Last Hunt**, **The Exile's Return**, **The Red Wizard's Price**, **An Audience with the Open Lord**, **Consulting Hlam**, **Smoke in the Tower**, **The Undercliff Scarecrows**, **The Doppelganger Problem**, **The Fouled Channel**, **Breaking Up the Brawl**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/order-of-the-gauntlet/01-savra-belabranta.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Trollskull Alley**, **Fireball!**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, **Kolat Towers**, **Vault of Dragons**, **Order of the Gauntlet First Meeting**, **Breaking Up the Brawl**, **The Black Viper Investigation**, **The Shard Shunners**, **Spined Devils in Twelvedog Court**, **The Shrine on Aveen Street**, **The Writ and the Weight**, **The Tithe**, **Savra's Past**, **Marcheon**, **Whitehawk**, **Vindicator**, **Righteous Hand**, Cassalanter Villa (location)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:1: # Xanathar
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:7: > - **Villain profile:** see **Xanathar** in Villains
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Vault of Dragons**; **Xanathar Sewer Hideout** (location)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:12: **Resonance.** Xanathar should inspire dread with its paranoid volatility, dark amusement with its obsessive love for Sylgar the goldfish, and discomfort when the party realizes that threatening the fish is both the easiest and the most dangerous leverage they have.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:14: **Emotions.** Xanathar most often feels paranoid suspicion, megalomaniacal self-importance, obsessive tenderness toward Sylgar, volcanic rage at perceived conspiracies, smug satisfaction, and a terror of mortality that it projects outward as aggression.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:16: **Motivations.** Xanathar wants to eliminate every Zhentarim operative in Waterdeep, recover the Stone of Golorr, and ensure that no one takes anything that belongs to it, especially Sylgar.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:18: **Inspirations.** When playing Xanathar, channel Tony Montana (*Scarface*), Smaug (*The Hobbit*), and GLaDOS (*Portal*).
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Xanathar is a paranoid, megalomaniacal beholder who charms and bullies minions into servitude. To those in its lair, it destroys a character and offers the survivors a bargain. Deep down, the only creature it truly cares about aside from itself is Sylgar the goldfish, kept in a glass tank while its minions live in terror of the fish dying and scramble to replace it. It cannot tell one fish from another. Its greatest weakness is its insane love for its pet; a DC 16 Intimidation check using Sylgar as leverage turns it into a blubbering mess.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:24: **Morale.** In a fight, Xanathar uses its eye rays without restraint. If reduced to half hit points or believing itself in mortal danger, it activates its ring of invisibility, screams for Ahmaergo to "tear the usurpers limb from limb," and flees to its sanctum.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:26: **Relationships.** Xanathar is the beholder crime lord of Xanathar's Guild, the employer of Ahmaergo (its majordomo), Nihiloor (its mind flayer, which has psionically dominated Xanathar for the last four months without Xanathar knowing), and Nar'l Xibrindas (its drow advisor, whom it does not yet know is Jarlaxle's spy). It has a healthy fear of Laeral Silverhand and spares those in her employ.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:30: Xanathar is a beholder who styles itself master of Waterdeep's criminal underworld, commanding a vast network of thieves, smugglers, and killers from a lair deep beneath the city. It is known for its paranoid volatility and its obsessive tenderness for a goldfish named Sylgar.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Vault of Dragons**, **The Compromised Eye**, **The Dive**, **Force Grey Mission 4 — Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:13: **Emotions.** Ahmaergo most often feels smug authority, methodical cruelty, possessive fascination with minotaurs, unflinching obedience to Xanathar, controlled satisfaction in discipline and order, and disdain for anyone weaker than himself.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:15: **Motivations.** Ahmaergo wants to maintain his position as the most influential member of the Guild after Xanathar, expand his minotaur collection, and run the Guild's operations with the efficiency that keeps the beholder's paranoia pointed at others.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Ahmaergo is outwardly civil, a shield dwarf who handles logistics, procurement, and discipline with administrative calm. To those in the Guild, he is as devious and corrupt as the worst devil, yet unflinchingly loyal to Xanathar. Deep down, his quarters feature a maze of stacked crates, a stuffed minotaur, skeletal remains arranged as decor, and a bed made from minotaur skulls, bones, hide, and fur. The fascination is total.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:23: **Morale.** In a fight, Ahmaergo commands Guild forces and fights with vicious competence. He does not retreat while Xanathar is present, and he responds to the beholder's screamed orders without hesitation.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:25: **Relationships.** Ahmaergo is Xanathar's majordomo (most orders that bear Xanathar's name were written by Ahmaergo), the Guild's de facto second-in-command, and a shield dwarf whose minotaur obsession fills every corner of his quarters.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:29: Ahmaergo is the outward face of Xanathar's Guild for administrative matters — a civil shield dwarf who manages procurement, discipline, and operations with methodical competence. He is as polite as he is dangerous.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild (secretly Bregan D'aerthe)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, **The Wazoo Affair**, **The Betrayal Pitch**, **The Compromised Eye**, **The Dive**, **Doom Raiders Mission 1 — The Dockside Killer**, **Force Grey Mission 4 — Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:13: **Emotions.** Nar'l most often feels nervous calculation, growing paranoia, practiced competence masking terror, contempt for Xanathar's delusions, desperate self-preservation, and bitter awareness that Jarlaxle would sacrifice him without hesitation if the cost were right.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:15: **Motivations.** Nar'l wants to survive his assignment as Jarlaxle's intelligence asset inside Xanathar's Guild, avoid Xanathar's suspicion (which is growing), and keep his options open in case he needs to betray Bregan D'aerthe to save himself.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Nar'l is Xanathar's trusted drow advisor who convinced the beholder to eliminate its other counselors. To Jarlaxle, he is Bregan D'aerthe's best intelligence source on Guild movements, reporting via Sending spell after one year in place, placed in 1491 DR. Deep down, Xanathar suspects a mole and has assigned Nar'l a grell bodyguard with orders to kill him at the first sign of disloyalty. If forced to choose between himself and Bregan D'aerthe, he will choose himself.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:25: **Relationships.** Nar'l is the younger brother of Soluun Xibrindas (who considers him a weakling), Jarlaxle's intelligence asset inside Xanathar's Guild, and Xanathar's only advisor, who has a grell bodyguard assigned to execute him at the first sign of treachery.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:29: Nar'l Xibrindas is a drow mage serving as Xanathar's only advisor, presenting himself as a trusted counselor who has the beholder's ear. He is precise, controlled, and carefully unremarkable.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **The Trouble with Meloon**, **Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:11: **Resonance.** Nihiloor should inspire visceral horror with its alien otherness, intellectual dread when the party learns how many people it has compromised, and helpless rage at the knowledge that it has bent Xanathar to its will and the beholder has no idea.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:15: **Motivations.** Nihiloor wants to breed and deploy intellect devourers throughout Waterdeep, pursue its own fell schemes independent of Xanathar's goals, and observe the Grand Game from within the shadows where no one looks.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Nihiloor is Xanathar's mind flayer operative. To Xanathar, it is a trusted mind flayer operative with a status no other Guild member holds. Deep down, Nihiloor has psionically dominated Xanathar for the last four months, and Xanathar does not know. It breeds intellect devourers, sets them loose in the sewers, and uses them to occupy Waterdavians, who walk the city with their own brains intact and a devourer steering them. Its methods leave little behind for PCs to question. Meloon Wardragon and Watch clerk Orvyn Dall are among its current hosts.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:25: **Relationships.** Nihiloor is the intelligence and infiltration specialist of Xanathar's Guild, the creator of the intellect devourer that occupies Meloon Wardragon, and a mind flayer that has dominated Xanathar's mind for the last four months in a Guild where even a hint of that would get anyone else disintegrated.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:29: Nihiloor is the intelligence operative of Xanathar's Guild — a mind flayer who moves through the lair with unsettling calm, treating every conversation as an opportunity for study. The Guild's other members give it wide berth.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:13: **Emotions.** Noska most often feels aggressive boredom, brute satisfaction in violence, simple loyalty to Xanathar, territorial possessiveness, flat disdain for weakness, and occasional contentment when feeding discarded weapons to his rust monster.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:15: **Motivations.** Noska wants to hurt people Xanathar tells him to hurt, maintain his position as the Guild's heaviest response option, and feed his rust monster.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Noska is a ruthless enforcer with a heavy crossbow replacing his left forearm (lost to a green slime in Undermountain). To those in the Guild, he is sent when Xanathar is done sending messages and wants the problem gone. Deep down, he keeps a rust monster as a pet and uses mannequins for target practice. He sleeps in a converted bathtub padded with straw and furs. His inner life is exactly as simple as it appears.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:25: **Relationships.** Noska is an enforcer of Xanathar's Guild, the third response team leader, and a shield dwarf whose crossbow-arm, rust monster pet, and bathtub bed constitute a complete portrait of his priorities.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:29: Noska Ur'gray is an enforcer of Xanathar's Guild, recognizable by the heavy crossbow that replaces his left forearm. He is direct, uncomplicated, and reliably present whenever the Guild needs a problem solved by violence.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Three Nights**, **Force Grey Mission 4 — Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:13: **Emotions.** Ott most often feels low-grade fear that this is the Sylgar who finally dies on his watch, the meditative calm of someone who has outsourced his existential crisis to a demon queen of fungi, absurd pride in the fish's consistent good health, and genuine relief when intruders arrive who are not from Xanathar.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:15: **Motivations.** Ott wants to keep Sylgar alive, avoid being blamed for anything Xanathar decides is someone's fault, and eventually find a Zuggtmoy shrine not located directly below a beholder's sanctum.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Ott Steeltoes is the dwarf with the beholder-eyestalk hat who tends the fish and sleeps under the bowl. To those who free him from the Interrogation House, he is grateful, terrified, and full of accurate information about Xanathar's Lair — including guard schedules, trap locations, and the fact that the large fishbowl weighs 6,000 pounds. Deep down, he has known for years that Xanathar will eventually blame him for something, and he is increasingly grateful to anyone who provides a reasonable exit.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:23: **Morale.** In a fight, Ott stammers, closes his eyes, and attempts to communicate his distress to Xanathar. This does not work. He then runs.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:25: **Relationships.** Ott is Xanathar's fishkeeper (all Sylgar replacements have passed through him), a Zuggtmoy worshipper who cultivates mushrooms in his quarters, a Zhentarim prisoner at the Interrogation House, a field agent of Xanathar's Guild, and a detailed intelligence source for anyone who rescues him and asks the right questions about the Lair. He is also the one who put Eye #1 of the Stone of Golorr in Sylgar's bowl as a pretty stone, and he has no idea what it is.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild (secretly Harpers)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:11: **Resonance.** Thorvin should inspire initial wariness with his mechanical work for Xanathar; growing sympathy when his reluctance becomes visible; and genuine trust when he turns out to be the inside man.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Thorvin is an irritable dwarf engineer building mechanical horrors for Xanathar and taking no questions about his methods. To those who earn his trust, he has been a Harper informant for three years, knows exactly where Nar'l Xibrindas hid the smokepowder, and will tell the party where to place it for maximum effect before he flees up the cellar stairs. Deep down, Xanathar has sent a gazer to watch him, and Thorvin knows it, and the gazer knows Thorvin knows it.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:25: **Relationships.** Thorvin is an engineer of Xanathar's Guild, a secret Harper informant, the man who maintains the Lair's mechanical constructs and traps (and knows every failure condition), and the reason the smokepowder demolition option is viable.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:29: Thorvin Twinbeard is an irritable dwarf engineer employed by Xanathar's Guild to maintain its mechanical constructs and devices. He takes no questions about his methods or his employers.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:5: > - *Tiefling priest (Xanathar's Guild), neutral evil, he/him*. Stat block: **Priest**.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:15: **Motivations.** Keln wants to run the Terasse Estate operation smoothly, provide Xanathar with a reliable healing and transport service, and avoid becoming interesting to anyone with authority.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Keln Trigos runs a quiet healing operation in a Castle Ward rowhouse and asks no questions about how you got hurt. To those who know the passphrases, the building runs two entirely separate services that have never been officially linked, and both function without incident. Deep down, he has worked for Xanathar long enough to understand what happens to people the Guild stops using, and he is therefore very reliable.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:23: **Morale.** In a fight, Keln uses priestly spells to hold ground, calls his guards, and sends a message to Xanathar. He does not run — the building is his responsibility.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:25: **Relationships.** Keln is the Tier 1 outpost keeper of Xanathar's Guild (Terasse Estate, Elsambul's Lane, Castle Ward), the manager of the gladiatorial transport route (healing services and Lair access under the same roof), and the keeper of two passphrases that access very different services.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/09-grinda-garloth.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Independent (contractor to Xanathar's Guild and Bregan D'aerthe)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/09-grinda-garloth.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/09-grinda-garloth.md:25: **Relationships.** Grinda Garloth is a black market armorer and salvage wizard at Mistshore, an allied service provider to both Xanathar's Guild and Bregan D'aerthe, the owner of the Apparatus of Kwalish, and an emergency combat support option in the Stone of Golorr investigation if the party defended her home.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:15: **Motivations.** Korgstrod wants to maintain discipline in the sewer hideout and earn Xanathar's favor by running the Orb Confectioners operation without trouble.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Korgstrod Uxgulm is a duergar in a stone chair attended by kobold lackeys who keep asking about a whip. To those who deal with him, he is the officer who runs Xanathar's Orb Confectioners hideout and expects obedience.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:25: **Relationships.** Korgstrod is the duergar leader of the Orb Confectioners sewer hideout, the second response team leader of Xanathar's Guild, and the commander of a kobold retinue whose continued employment is contingent on not being in his field of vision when things go wrong.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/11-gorath.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/11-gorath.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/11-gorath.md:25: **Relationships.** Gorath is the first response team leader of Xanathar's Guild (four duergar, sent when the Guild wants a lesson taught rather than a person killed), the formal notice that Xanathar knows what the party did, and the first escalation before Korgstrod's team and then Noska's arrive.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/12-grevik-nass.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/12-grevik-nass.md:15: **Motivations.** Grevik wants to compile an accurate patron intelligence file for Xanathar's Guild (who dines here, with whom, and about what), make the food worth recommending so no one looks closely at the cook, and complete the assignment without drawing a replacement.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/12-grevik-nass.md:25: **Relationships.** Grevik is an operative of Xanathar's Guild embedded as the tavern's Master Cook candidate, a man whose two-tenday file will give Xanathar a usable picture of Trollskull's patronage, and one of two Guild plants in the tavern staff alongside Tobrin Ashvale, whose position behind the bar gives the Guild a second angle on the same room.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/13-tobrin-ashvale.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/13-tobrin-ashvale.md:25: **Relationships.** Tobrin is an operative of Xanathar's Guild embedded as the tavern's bartender, one of the Guild's two Trollskull infiltrators alongside Grevik Nass, and the trigger for the Guild's escalating response conditions (detailed in the **Trollskull Manor** guide); when the party crosses a threshold, Tobrin makes the call.

campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:1: # Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:7: > - **Villain profile:** see **Xanathar** in Villains
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:12: Xanathar is a beholder, paranoid, impulsive, and absolute in its conviction that everyone around it is either a tool or a threat. It holds territory through fear and loyalty through power, two things it understands. Trust, it doesn't understand at all.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:14: Its most visible eccentricity is Sylgar, its pet goldfish. Xanathar's attachment to the fish is total and terrifying. The Guild's agents who let anything bad happen to Sylgar can expect to be eaten. This is not a metaphor.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:16: Its agenda in the Grand Game is territorial. Xanathar held the Stone of Golorr until Dalakhar stole it; it still holds one of the Stone's Eyes. The gold itself holds little appeal; the leverage it represents is everything. Xanathar wants the Stone recovered and the vault kept hidden — a source of leverage it can threaten but never has to use.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:20: - **Xanathar** — Beholder crime lord, lawful evil. See the **Xanathar** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:21: - **Ahmaergo** (DH p. 195) — Shield dwarf majordomo, lawful evil. Ahmaergo handles logistics: gladiatorial procurement, outpost resupply, discipline. Most orders that bear Xanathar's name are written by Ahmaergo. See the **Ahmaergo** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:22: - **Nar'l Xibrindas** (DH p. 213) — Drow mage, neutral evil. Xanathar's advisor and secret Bregan D'aerthe asset. Nar'l reports to Jarlaxle by *Sending* spell and has been operating in place for one year, placed in 1491 DR. The smokepowder delivery referenced in the Korgstrod correspondence was his operation. See the **Nar'l Xibrindas** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:23: - **Nihiloor** (DH p. 213) — Mind flayer, lawful evil. Officially handles Xanathar's most sensitive intelligence operations. In practice, Nihiloor is running three concurrent projects: (1) **The Infiltration** — intellect devourers placed inside city government officials give Nihiloor eyes throughout Waterdeep's bureaucracy; three Harper assets are already compromised; (2) **The Experiments** — creatures captured during Arena operations are being subjected to psionic modification seeking a "relay organism" that extends the colony's reach without destroying the host brain; (3) **The Puppet** — Nihiloor has been exerting psionic domination over Xanathar itself for the past four months, replacing its strategic decisions while leaving its personality intact. Evidence exists in Nihiloor's research chamber in the deep lair; its long-term payoff is the Seadeeps colony (Undermountain Level 17). See the **Nihiloor** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:24: - **Noska Ur'gray** (DH p. 213) — Shield dwarf enforcer, neutral evil. Xanathar's heaviest response option. Leads the third response team when earlier escalations fail. See the **Noska Ur'gray** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:25: - **Ott Steeltoes** — Dwarf cult fanatic (Zuggtmoy), neutral evil. Guild field agent, currently held prisoner in the Zhentarim Interrogation House. Wears a leather skullcap stitched with fake beholder eyestalks. If rescued and cultivated, he can lead the PCs toward Xanathar's Lair. See the **Ott Steeltoes** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:27: - **Keln Trigos** — Tiefling priest (Xanathar's Guild), neutral evil. Stationed at the Terasse Estate. Provides healing for the Guild's agents and manages the gladiatorial audience transports to the Lair. See the **Keln Trigos** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:28: - **Grinda Garloth** — Illuskan human mage (black market armorer), chaotic neutral. Black market armorer based at Mistshore. Allied with Xanathar; equips the Guild's agents and rents equipment to third parties, including (per the documents) Bregan D'aerthe. See the **Grinda Garloth** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:36: - **Xanathar's Guild** (Factions guide): response teams, outposts, escalation, revelation list.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:40: Xanathar's Guild is Waterdeep's largest criminal syndicate, run by a paranoid beholder from a fortified lair below the city. The Guild controls access to Skullport's smuggling networks, gladiatorial circuits, and a web of informants embedded throughout the wards. Its field operations are coordinated by Ahmaergo, and its three city outposts — a healer's clinic, a black market armorer, and a sweetshop front — form the chain that leads investigators toward the lair.
campaign/setting/the-grand-game/the-grand-game.md:29: - **Informants:** The Watch has informants in every ward `[research]`, and every faction has informants in the Watch `[research]`. Jarlaxle has informants in every other faction `[research]`, and two of them are sourced. Nar'l Xibrindas, Xanathar's drow advisor, reports to him by *sending* spell, and Vessa, a doppelganger, circulates in the Cassalanters' social circle. Skeemo Weirdbottle, the Doom Raiders' Master of Magic, has been bought by House Gralhund and the Black Network operatives loyal to Manshoon, and he sends messages by paper bird both to his new friends and to his old ones.

campaign/setting/villains/jarlaxle.md:57: - Eye #3 began in **Renaer Neverember**'s mourning locket. Manshoon's Zhentarim seized the locket the night before Ches 1 and pried it open at the Candle Lane warehouse. Xanathar's Guild raided the warehouse and carried off the Eye with the rest of the back office without knowing what it was, and Bregan D'aerthe agents already inside Xanathar's hideout lifted it that night. It reached Jarlaxle before the party reached the hideout, and Manshoon's cell never held it (**Running the Villains**; the **Xanathar Sewer Hideout** location journal, area overview). He keeps it in his stateroom, U4, aboard the *Scarlet Marpenoth* (the **Sea Maidens Faire** location journal, area overview).
campaign/setting/villains/jarlaxle.md:58: - **Nar'l Xibrindas** is Xanathar's trusted drow advisor and secretly Jarlaxle's best intelligence source on the Guild. He reports by *Sending* spell, and if forced to choose between himself and Bregan D'aerthe, he will choose himself (the **Nar'l Xibrindas** page).

campaign/setting/villains/jarlaxle.md:121: - **Hold one Eye of the Stone.** He wants it as a bargaining chip, not for personal use. Method: he already holds Eye #3 aboard the *Scarlet Marpenoth* (Bregan D'aerthe agents inside Xanathar's hideout lifted it the night Xanathar's Guild raided the Zhentarim warehouse), and Nar'l's intelligence tells him where the other Eyes are. He keeps it as the bargaining chip and moves on the others before the other factions consolidate. Contingency: negotiating it from the PCs is also acceptable and preferable for the relationship. Connects to **Sea Maidens Faire**.

campaign/setting/villains/manshoon.md:71: - The war with Xanathar is an active, low-level street war in the Dock Ward. Xanathar's kill order on Manshoon has failed twice, and Manshoon knows Xanathar killed his envoys and kept Eye #1.
campaign/setting/villains/manshoon.md:76: - In the published adventure, characters who reach him are offered a place in his organization if they kill Xanathar. He does not mean to keep the promise.
campaign/setting/villains/manshoon.md:80: - **Acquire the Stone of Golorr.** The Stone is a transformed aboleth encoded with ancient Weave knowledge, and Manshoon means to absorb the Weave and supplant Mystra. The vault's gold funds the process. Method: Stone recovery, then Weave absorption. He holds no Eye, because Xanathar killed the envoys who delivered Eye #1 as a gift and kept it, so he needs the Stone plus all three Eyes. Contingency: destroy the vault rather than cede it. Connects to **Gralhund Villa** and **Kolat Towers**.
campaign/setting/villains/manshoon.md:81: - **Control Waterdeep's Underworld.** He means to eliminate Xanathar, absorb the Doom Raiders and marginalize Bregan D'aerthe. Method: patient erosion through targeted disruption and strategic violence. The Dock Ward conflict in Acts I–II is this plan in early operation.

campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:1: # Xanathar
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:7: > - This is the villain profile for Xanathar, the beholder crime lord of **Xanathar's Guild**. It sets out its three personality phases, its relationships, its goals and the one design note that makes it playable.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:8: > - Use it when you run any scene where Xanathar or the Guild acts or reacts. Phase 1 covers Acts I and II, Phase 2 covers Act III, and Phase 3 begins when the party enters its lair.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:9: > - Xanathar appears in **Finding Floon**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair** and **Vault of Dragons**. The **Xanathar Sewer Hideout** is the location journal for its sewer base.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:10: > - The Guild's response teams, outposts, escalation row and revelation list live in the **Xanathar's Guild** page of the Factions Guide. Who the Guild is lives in the **Xanathar's Guild** page of Organizations.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:12: **Purpose:** Lets the GM portray Xanathar as a specific, three-dimensional villain through three personality phases, a relationship table and three goals.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:16: Xanathar is a beholder and the crime lord of **Xanathar's Guild**, a paranoid intelligence that has built a criminal empire around a terror of being vulnerable and wears the trappings of a monster because the trappings are convenient. Its core motive is to prove its supremacy and remove every threat to it, which means taking the Vault of Dragons, destroying Manshoon and keeping Skullport's money flowing.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:20: - "Xanathar" is a title, derived from the personal name of the Guild's founder and held by the leaders who came after (Forgotten Realms Wiki, Xanathar (title)). The founder established the criminal underworld's structure, and each later Xanathar inherited the apparatus and the paranoia together.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:21: - The Guild keeps continuity by systematically eliminating anyone who knows more than the current Xanathar. After about 135 years of this, the Guild is a surveillance network with no institutional memory beyond what the current beholder chooses to believe.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:22: - The current Xanathar is a beholder named Zushaxx, and its pronouns are "it". Kirukesai was one of the previous Xanathars. The user supplied both names. The Forgotten Realms Wiki confirms them: Zushaxx is the Xanathar of 1492 DR, and Kirukeskai claimed the title in the 14th century, in 1372 DR (the wiki spells it Kirukeskai; the campaign spells it Kirukesai).
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:23: - The current Xanathar believes it is the original. This belief is now structural to the Guild's identity rather than correctable. How the belief arose is not settled in any source, and the user has settled it ("Remix overtakes original campaign"): the remix line wins over the published adventure's area X33, where Xanathar gazes somberly at the tombs of its predecessors.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:35: | 3 | The Cornered Monster | **Xanathar's Lair** | Volcanic and occasionally lucid; all safeguards failed; the goldfish is the only consistent anchor |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:47: | Ahmaergo | Majordomo | Most trusted lieutenant; the only one Xanathar registers as competent; Ahmaergo knows he is replaceable |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:49: | Nihiloor | Mind flayer operative | Trusted intelligence gatherer; Xanathar grants it unusual autonomy; plant for Mad Mage integration |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:50: | Manshoon | Rival | Active enemy; Xanathar has ordered Manshoon killed twice; both failed; this is accelerating the paranoia |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:52: | The PCs | Threat | Irritants → active problem → something requiring Xanathar's personal attention (rarely good for anyone) |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:55: - Nihiloor has been psionically dominating Xanathar for four months, so some decisions attributed to Xanathar are Nihiloor's (**Xanathar's Lair** structure notes, and the **Xanathar's Guild** organization page).
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:56: - Xanathar suspects Nar'l of disloyalty and has set a grell to kill him at the first proof. It does not know he reports to Bregan D'aerthe or to Jarlaxle.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:61: - **Acquire the Vault of Dragons.** It wants the gold as proof of supremacy and of its rule over the underworld, not primarily for wealth. It knows the Stone is the key to the vault, but not what the Eye in Sylgar's bowl does, that there are three Eyes, or where the vault is. Method: recover the Stone and whatever it unlocks. Contingency: pursue whoever has it. Connects to **Xanathar's Lair**.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:67: Xanathar's goldfish Sylgar receives more consideration than any humanoid lieutenant. The GM should establish this early — it becomes a specific vulnerability and a specific dramatic moment if Sylgar is harmed during the lair heist.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:71: Xanathar's Guild is the largest criminal organization in Waterdeep, and the only one run by a beholder. Most Waterdhavians know it by rumor, as a smuggling trade through Skullport and a web of informants in every ward, with the Dock Ward as its main territory above ground. Its master, Xanathar, is said to rule from a fortified lair deep beneath the streets and to trust nobody who works for it. It is also known for an obsessive fondness for a goldfish named Sylgar.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:73: **Tone:** Plain and procedural for the GM tables, and grave in the Overview, where the Guild is a frightening local fact. Treat Xanathar's paranoia as dangerous and the goldfish as funny without letting the narration make the joke.

campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:110: > - **Routed facts:** Zushaxx as the current Xanathar, Kirukesai as a previous one, and the "it" pronoun belong on the **Xanathar** Notable Figure page and the Guild organization page, which still use "he/his" in places.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:15: Ten outposts are distributed across Waterdeep's wards, maintained by four factions, each one a data point in the web of the Grand Game. The party cannot storm the lairs blind — Xanathar's sanctum, Cassalanter Villa's temple, the Sea Maidens Faire, and Kolat Towers are not targets until the party knows enough to attempt them. The outposts are the research: entering a Xanatharian front to find how the Guild accesses its own lair, or breaking into a cultist's apartment to find a map the Cassalanters spent months assembling.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:35: That arrangement has fallen apart. Neverember is in Neverwinter, and none of the three Eyes is where he left it. Jarlaxle Baenre holds the Eye from Renaer's locket aboard the *Scarlet Marpenoth*, where it serves as leverage and curiosity in equal measure. Manshoon's cell seized the locket the night before Ches 1, Xanathar's Guild raided the Candle Lane warehouse where it was opened, and Bregan D'aerthe agents lifted the Eye from the Guild's hideout before dawn. The Cassalanter family, working through a network of cult agents and infernal diviners, located the Eye in the crypt near Alethea Brandath's tomb and extracted it. Xanathar holds the Neverwinter Eye in Sylgar's fishbowl in the lair beneath Skullport. Zhentarim envoys stole it from the Protector's Enclave in Neverwinter and carried it to Xanathar as a gift. Xanathar killed them, kept the stone, and has never learned what it is.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:37: Manshoon holds no Eye. His envoys carried one to Xanathar and never came back. He wants the Stone itself — specifically, the compressed aboleth memory within it, which encodes structural knowledge of the Weave that Mystra's servants have never catalogued. The vault is an instrument he would use to fund his actual goal. The Stone is the goal. He needs the Stone and all three Eyes, but he never intended to open the vault the way Neverember did — only to hold the Stone long enough to extract what he needs.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:39: The Stone now sits with the party. The Watch crackdown following the Gralhund Villa incident has disrupted Manshoon's Brindul Alley operation, scattered the Zhentarim response teams, and landed Davil Starsong in Watch custody on circumstantial evidence. Xanathar's Guild is consolidating — pulling agents tighter, increasing surveillance. Jarlaxle watches the party with quiet interest and no urgency; he holds something they need, and he is in no hurry to discuss terms. The Cassalanters, undisturbed, continue their research. Below the city's peaceful surface, four factions know the Stone has surfaced. Three of them want it back.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:63: *Stone Knowledge Expansion (Forward Note — Implement in Heist Arcs).* The Alexandrian Remix establishes that the Stone's knowledge expands incrementally as each Eye is restored. With zero Eyes, it yields only the fragmentary impressions above. With one Eye, it reveals additional details. With two, more still. All three Eyes unlock the vault's precise location and command phrase. This expansion belongs in the lair heists (**Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**) where Eyes are actually recovered — not in this quest. This note exists so the mechanic is not overlooked when those arcs are drafted. Cross-ref `sources/8. The Eyes of the Stone.pdf` for the authoritative expansion table.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:81: *Mirt (Harpers — renown 1+).* The Harpers have been watching the Cassalanter family for years, and Mirt's summary reflects sustained, inconclusive surveillance. The Cassalanters give more to charity than houses twice their wealth, never miss a temple service, and have not made a single political enemy in a decade. "Nobody is that clean," Mirt says, without pleasure. He does not know what they are hiding. The Harpers have found no evidence of wrongdoing — only the complete absence of any wrongdoing at all, which in Waterdeep's political ecology is itself suspicious. He recommends starting with Xanathar outposts on the grounds that the Guild's paranoia makes early strikes more effective than late ones. His unstated reason: the Harpers have already exhausted their Xanathar intelligence, and the Cassalanter question is the one he cannot answer and cannot let go.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:115: **Xanathar's Guild:** Grinda Garloth's operates openly in Mistshore as a black-market armorer. Anyone asking about Xanathar weapons or Guild suppliers in the Dock Ward gets pointed to her within a day. No credentials required.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:127: *Cross-Faction Spillover.* Xanathar and Manshoon are actively at war; each monitors the other's losses. Hitting a Xanathar outpost raises Manshoon's alert tier by 1, and vice versa — both factions assume the other is responsible before they conclude it was the party. Cassalanter imp surveillance of the Sea Maidens Faire creates a reciprocal intelligence channel; Cassalanter losses alert Jarlaxle that something is moving against infernal-adjacent operations in the city. See **Running the Villains** (GM Guide) for full inter-faction dynamics.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:135: ### Scene 4: Xanathar's Guild Outposts
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:137: **Purpose:** Present three Xanathar outposts as a linked intelligence chain, each one peeling back a layer of organized crime — a gentleman's clinic, an arms dealership, a confectioners' front over a duergar smuggling cellar. All three feed into **Xanathar's Lair**.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:147: *Step 1 — Gather Information.* Three paths to Terasse Estate before the party enters it: the *Instructions for Gladiatorial Weaponry* found at Grinda Garloth's (names Terasse as a delivery point for exotic arms); the mechanical beholder letter at Orb Confectioners (names Terasse as the delivery address and dates it); or, if **Cassalanter Villa** has been completed first, a Cassalanter Villa report referencing the gladiatorial supply chain. Parties lacking these documents can attempt entry by bluffing past the door with the healing passphrase (DC 18 Deception if improvising) or by obtaining the passphrase from an interrogated Xanathar response team member.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:149: *Step 2 — Case the Target.* A modest, unremarkable rowhouse. Two kinds of visitors come: those seeking healing (legitimate front), and those attending gladiatorial events in Xanathar's Lair. Two passphrases control entry: "I heard the red hand was hot" accesses the healing operation; "the bawdy star shines bright tonight" gets a guest past the front rooms. See the **Xanathar's Guild** Factions guide page for the full area layout.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:153: *Step 4 — The Operation.* Primary objectives: recover the *Instructions for Gladiatorial Weaponry* from Keln's desk (Area 6–7), which names Grinda Garloth as the supplier and Ahmaergo as the authorizing agent; recover the *Injury Reports* logbook (Area 5 Upper Landing), which contains an entry noting a wound caused by "incautious observation of the windmill on Coachlamp Lane" — a cross-faction clue pointing toward the Cassalanter outpost chain; recover the *Ill Man's Note* (Area 8–10 Sick Rooms), which contains the Orb Confectioners passphrase in verse. Optional: follow a guest being transported to the gladiatorial event. The trap door in the kitchen leads to an underground dock; guests are blindfolded before descent and transported by boat to Area X4 of Xanathar's Lair. This route is a genuine lair entry method, but arriving via gladiatorial transport means arriving unprepared, blindfolded, and unarmed. See the **Xanathar's Guild** Factions guide page for keyed area details.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:155: *Step 5 — Getaway.* Keln Trigos (priest) and his guards raise the alarm when any objective area is breached: the trap door seals from below, the front door bars, and a flying snake courier carries word to the Lair. The alarm window is 1d4+2 rounds before lockdown. **Escalation: Xanathar moves to Suspicious (Tier 2).** If a second Xanathar outpost has already been hit, Xanathar moves directly to Alert (Tier 3) and Gorath's response team deploys to the last known party location within 2 days.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:163: *Dock Ward, Mistshore.* — **Street-level entry point for the Xanathar chain.**
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:165: *Approach.* Grinda operates openly as a black-market armorer. Anyone asking about Xanathar weapons, Guild suppliers, or black-market arms in the Dock Ward gets pointed to Mistshore within a day (DC 12 Charisma in Dock Ward taverns or street contacts; no faction membership required). Three additional paths: the *Instructions for Gladiatorial Weaponry* from Terasse Estate names her; a captured Xanathar response team member's *Directive from Nar'l* names her as a supplier; Agorn Fuoco's *Report to Agorn Fuoco* (from any Zhentarim response team) confirms her location. Parties can approach as customers, investigators, or raiders. Grinda does not fight for the Guild — she fights for her business. She will negotiate if the party offers more immediate value than Xanathar does, and she will report the encounter afterward.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:167: *Key Areas.* Area D2: 500 gp and the *Note Regarding Gladiatorial Weaponry* from Ahmaergo, confirming delivery to Terasse Estate and citing Xanathar's expectation of "exotic gutting." Area D3: mundane arms and armor including single-shot pistols; 1d4 pieces of magic arms or armor (DMG Table F); 1d4 miscellaneous magic items (DMG Table A or B). See the **Xanathar's Guild** Factions guide page for full area descriptions.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:169: *Intel Yield.* The Ahmaergo correspondence confirms the Terasse connection and establishes Ahmaergo as a named Guild lieutenant. Grinda's knowledge of sewer patrol routes is available through negotiation or by locating her operational logbook — partial maps of Xanathar's Lair approach corridors. The magic item inventory is genuine loot.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:171: **Escalation: Xanathar moves to Suspicious (Tier 2) if this outpost is raided; no tier change if the party engages as customers without taking documents.** Response teams do not deploy from a single customer interaction. **Founders' Day Clock: 1–2 days.**
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:175: #### 4C: Orb Confectioners / Xanathar Sewer Hideout (Encounter-Plus)
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:181: *Approach.* Three paths to Orb Confectioners: a captured Xanathar response team member carries the passphrase or the location in their directive; Agorn Fuoco's *Report to Agorn Fuoco* names Orb Confectioners on Rising Ride; Terasse Estate's *Ill Man's Note* contains the passphrase in verse. The passphrase exchange: ask for "eight small eyes." The counter-staff responds: "How would you like those arranged?" Answer: "Around an ocular cake." Correct answers earn an escort to the cellar stairs. Use the Autumn Cellar Complex layout (DH p. 86). See the **Xanathar's Guild** organization page.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:183: *Key Areas.* Area B7: *On the Delivery of a Mechanical Beholder* from Ahmaergo, setting a delivery deadline of Tarsakh 9th for a mechanical beholder to Terasse Estate — confirms the gladiatorial supply chain and ties Ahmaergo to Xanathar's engineering operations. Area B9b: a **mimic** disguised as the chest at the foot of Korgstrod's bed. A hidden coffer under the bed (DC 13 to find) holds 500 gp and *Correspondence with Nar'l Xibrindas*: Nar'l's letter references smokepowder and provides guildsign directions toward Area X1 of Xanathar's Lair. The guildsign trail requires DC 15 checks, rising to DC 17 near the lair itself; three failures before a success means the trail is lost. See the **Xanathar's Guild** organization page.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:185: *Intel Yield.* Nar'l's correspondence is the primary entry path to Xanathar's Lair via the guildsign trail. The smokepowder reference seeds Nar'l's double-agent status — he is also in contact with Jarlaxle, a fact that pays off in **Xanathar's Lair** and **Sea Maidens Faire** when the BD-Xanathar connection surfaces.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:187: **Escalation: Xanathar moves to Alert (Tier 3) if this outpost is cleared.** Korgstrod's response — invisibility, enlargement, and four duergar fighting to the death — signals this location is more important than the others. Response teams deploy within 1d4 days of the operation. **Founders' Day Clock: 1–3 days.**
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:209: *Ott Steeltoes.* A gnome prisoner wearing a leather skullcap stitched with fake beholder eyestalks — an Xanatharian agent captured during the Guild war. Ott does not know what the Stone of Golorr is, but he knows Xanathar's Lair. If rescued and cultivated, he offers to introduce the party to the Guild or guide them personally to the lair's entrance. If freed without follow-through, he disappears back into Xanathar's network. See the **Ott Steeltoes** page in Notable Figures and the **Xanathar's Guild** organization page.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:211: *Intel Yield.* Manshoon's name (first use in the campaign). The pass-amulet from Avareen (route to Kolat Towers). Ott Steeltoes as a potential **Xanathar's Lair** contact. The *Directive to Zorbog* naming Fenerus Stormcastle — a cross-faction thread pointing toward Bregan D'aerthe (Fenerus is a BD lamplighter; Manshoon has identified him as a soft target).
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:213: **Escalation: Manshoon moves to Alert (Tier 3).** Avareen's flying snakes carry the alarm faster than the party can move; if even one escapes, Manshoon knows within hours. Vevette Blackwater's team deploys within 1d4 days. If Xanathar cross-faction spillover applies, Manshoon moves directly to Lockdown (Tier 4). **Founders' Day Clock: 1–2 days.**
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:327: *Step 1 — Gather Information.* Four paths to the theater as a BD outpost rather than a public venue: Fenerus's decoded messages (theater play titles serve as coded correspondence; one decoded message lists a stage address resolving to a dressing room number); a Xanathar's Lair report naming the Seven Masks Theater as a BD surface operation (available after **Xanathar's Lair**); Soluun Xibrindas's *Bloodstained Playbill* recovered from a BD response team, marked with a seat number pointing to the safehouse dressing room; or public theater investigation — noticing that one dressing room is never listed on the cast sheet.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:351: *Response Team Compositions.* See the relevant Factions guide pages for full compositions and carried documents. Every team leader carries faction-specific documents that function as intelligence opportunities: Zhentarim teams carry directives naming Brindul Alley or Kolat Towers; Xanathar teams carry operational correspondence from Nar'l or Ahmaergo; BD teams carry playbills or coded messages in Drow; Cassalanter teams carry instructions from Lord Cassalanter. The party should treat every response team encounter as a potential document recovery, not just a combat encounter.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:353: *Cross-Faction Response Team Encounters.* Xanathar and Manshoon teams may encounter each other during this quest — both factions are moving through the same wards. Witnessing these confrontations without engaging either side generates intelligence about both factions simultaneously. BD teams prefer misdirection over lethal force; they are more likely to shadow the party than confront them directly. Cassalanter teams vary by the party's relationship with the family.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:355: *The Cassalanter Dual Mode.* The Cassalanters respond differently depending on whether the party has accepted Ammalia's **Fireball!** dinner invitation and maintained a working relationship. Working with the Cassalanters: Seffia's team assists or at minimum does not interfere. Opposing or refusing them: imps maintain constant surveillance, Seffia's team competes at **Xanathar's Lair** operations, and Willifort Crowelle makes social approaches that escalate to assassination attempts if the party actively works against the family. See the **Cassalanters** Factions guide page (Response Teams section).
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:369: *Mirt (Harpers).* Full intelligence briefing. If Xanathar outposts were hit, Mirt asks specifically about Nihiloor references — the Harper file on the mind flayer is extensive, and any new document strengthens the case for Watch action. If Cassalanter outposts were hit and the party brings the Mirror of the Fiend sigil identification, the Cassalanter cult documents, or the cultists' testimony, Mirt's response is: relief, then urgency. He has been building suspicion for years without evidence. What the party has found in one outpost run is more concrete than everything he gathered in three years of surveillance. "I knew it couldn't just be charity." He does not have enough to move on the Cassalanters legally — he needs **Cassalanter Villa** for that — but the calculus on Founders' Day has changed.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:393: *Completing a chain:* Hit at least two outposts of the same faction and recover at least one piece of actionable lair intelligence. For Xanathar (three outposts), two of three is sufficient. For all other factions (two outposts), both must be cleared. A party that hits one Xanathar outpost and one Zhentarim outpost has started two chains but completed neither.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:395: Total **Faction Outposts**: 1 Milestone Point, for the first completed chain; a second chain earns intelligence, not points. The party remains 4th level through all of **Faction Outposts**, at 10 cumulative points. A party can attempt any lair heist (**Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, or **Kolat Towers**) after completing just one chain, or even none; this quest's outposts are the recommended on-ramp, not a hard gate. See the Milestone section in **Xanathar's Lair** (or any lair heist's) for how the heist phase carries the party from 4th to 7th level.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:397: *Transition.* The party holds enough intelligence to attempt at least one lair heist. The GM reviews each faction's escalation tier and communicates it through environmental detail: Dock Ward patrols are heavier than last tenday; a Castle Ward merchant notes that the old tower on the hill has gone dark; the Sea Maidens Faire hasn't performed in a week. Party chooses which lair first. **Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, and **Kolat Towers** are available in any order.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:421: 9. **Chain completion defined.** Two outposts of the same faction plus one piece of actionable lair intelligence. The first completed chain awards 1 Milestone Point (cumulative 10), not enough to reach level 5. Level 5 is reached on the first completed lair heist (**Xanathar's Lair**, **Cassalanter Villa**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, or **Kolat Towers**).
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:449: - **Xanathar's Lair** — Xanathar outpost chain (Scene 4) feeds directly into **Xanathar's Lair**. Nar'l's guildsign trail, the gladiatorial transport route, and Ott Steeltoes all function as **Xanathar's Lair** entry methods.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:461: ***The Problem of Non-Linear Content in a Linear Format.*** Every quest through **Gralhund Villa** moved in a single investigative current — a question asked, an investigation run, a confrontation resolved. **Faction Outposts** cannot function this way. Ten outposts in any order, at the party's discretion, cannot be scripted into a sequence without defeating the point. The nine-scene architecture is the solution: three framing scenes that run in order (Stone attunement, faction consultation, operational briefing), four faction sections that function as a menu, and two closing scenes that apply after the menu has been used. The faction sections are labeled as scenes to maintain the document's structural vocabulary — each has a Purpose, Content, and Tone — but the GM should understand that "Scene 4" does not mean "fourth in sequence." It means "this is the Xanathar section."
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:469: ***Cross-Faction Spillover and the Xanathar–Manshoon War.*** The spillover rules — hitting Xanathar raises Manshoon's tier and vice versa — have a specific purpose beyond realism. They ensure that a party pursuing only one faction's chain still generates meaningful pressure across multiple fronts. A party that focuses entirely on Xanathar outposts will encounter Manshoon response teams without having engaged Manshoon directly, surfacing the inter-faction war as lived experience rather than exposition.
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:491: ***Lair Entrance Verification.*** Four lair entrances, all reachable from this quest's intelligence: Xanathar's Lair — Nar'l's guildsign trail, gladiatorial transport via Terasse, Ott Steeltoes as guide (three paths); Kolat Towers — Yellowspire teleportation circle, captured pass-amulet from Avareen, Agorn's amulet traded under duress (three paths); Cassalanter Villa — Ammalia's **Fireball!** dinner invitation, Cassalanter cultist testimony from this quest's outposts, Esvele Rosznar in **Cassalanter Villa** (three paths, the last requiring no completion of this quest at all); Sea Maidens Faire — Fenerus's decoded messages, Malkolm Brizzenbright's testimony, Xanathar's Lair report from **Xanathar's Lair** (three paths).
campaign/structure/arc-e-faction-outposts.md:495: **Harpers.** This quest's Harper hook is an ongoing operative benefit rather than a single scene beat. Once the party reaches Renown 30+, Mirt can direct embedded Harper informants to provide advance intelligence against Xanathar and Manshoon outposts — shift rotations, guard counts, unusual personnel — delivered before each operation rather than discovered during it. A separate informant inside the Sea Maidens Faire reports that Jarlaxle has been asking about the party by description; Mirt passes this to Harper PCs as a quiet heads-up in the days before **Sea Maidens Faire** begins. These benefits are not surfaced in Scene 2's consultation; they activate through maintained renown and are delivered as one informant report at the start of each outpost operation the party undertakes in Scenes 4–7, once a Harper PC has Renown 30+. See the Quest Hooks section of the **Harpers** Factions guide page.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:1: # Xanathar's Lair
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:9: Three months before the Trollskull fireball, Manshoon dispatched two Zhentarim lieutenants to Skullport carrying a gift: Eye #1 of the Stone of Golorr, stolen from the Protector's Enclave in Neverwinter. The Eye was offered as a peace overture — proof that Manshoon's splinter faction could be a valuable partner in the Grand Game. Xanathar received the envoys in its throne room, listened to their proposal, and ate them. It kept the Eye and has never learned what it is. The Eye passed to Ott Steeltoes, who placed it in Sylgar's fishbowl because it had a pretty color and Xanathar liked Sylgar's bowl to look interesting.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:11: The lair beneath Skullport has been sealed for weeks. Since the fireball disrupted the Street of Silver trade routes, Xanathar has withdrawn into paranoia — convinced that one of the other Grand Game factions has mapped the lair's entrances. Ahmaergo runs external operations while Xanathar broods in the Sanctum, running its eye rays across the empty room and interrogating Guild members whose faces it does not immediately recognize. The gladiatorial games continue because the arena revenue continues, and because stopping them would signal weakness. But Xanathar does not attend from its throne as it once did. It watches from invisibility, alone.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:13: Nihiloor has used this paranoia as cover. The illithid's three unauthorized projects — running in parallel for years — are now approaching critical phases. The Infiltration: intellect devourers inserted into four positions in Waterdeep's Watch and civic administration, with two additional placements pending. The Experiments: twelve captives from the gladiatorial arena modified into psionic relay organisms, intended to form the backbone of a city-wide intelligence network that would answer only to Nihiloor. The Puppet: four months ago, Nihiloor successfully applied Dominate Monster to Xanathar through a sustained psionic relay device in X26. The Xanathar who has been running the Guild since then — the Xanathar who killed Manshoon's envoys, who sent response teams against the party's allies in **Faction Outposts**, who holds the Eye in a fishbowl because it trusts Ott's aesthetic judgment — has been Nihiloor's instrument. The beholder does not know this. The domination does not feel like domination; it feels like its own decisions.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:15: The panopticus surveillance network — five ghostly beholder eyestalks wired through enchanted crystal to a monitoring station at X16 — was Nihiloor's gift to Xanathar three years ago. The beholder believes the network serves its paranoia. Nihiloor built a structural back door into the control array from the beginning.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:23: **Purpose.** The party holds the Stone of Golorr and knows an Eye is somewhere in Xanathar's lair; that much was established in **Faction Outposts**. This scene converts that knowledge into operational intelligence. Seven faction contacts provide lair-specific information, each from a different angle on the same target. By the scene's end, the party has at least one confirmed entry method, two potential inside contacts, and enough threat assessment to plan an approach.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:31: *Mirt (Harpers — renown 1+).* Thorvin Twinbeard is inside the lair — a Harper asset embedded in the lair's engineering staff for six years, Mirt's most carefully kept secret. Mirt provides Thorvin's location (X13, the workshop) and a recognition code: ask him what he thinks of the new pulley design. His response — "It'll hold if the load is distributed right" — confirms the channel. If Xanathar outposts were hit in **Faction Outposts**, Mirt warns that Thorvin's cover may be compromised: a gazer has been assigned to watch him, and Thorvin knows it. At renown 10+, Mirt arranges a Harper field agent — use the spy stat block — to stage a timed surface distraction above the guildsign entrance: a staged brawl that occupies Guild topside watchers for 20 minutes at a prearranged signal.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:51: **Purpose.** Steps 1 through 3 of the heist framework: Identify the Score, Gather Information, Onsite Surveillance. The party transitions from intelligence briefing to direct reconnaissance of the lair. This scene establishes the three entry methods, maps the panopticus challenge, and forces the party to decide how to handle Xanathar's location before the operation begins.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:55: *Identifying the Score.* The Stone of Golorr's impression — a beholder's eye, unblinking in darkness, carved stone wet from underground springs, a long way down — combined with **Faction Outposts** outpost documents confirms the Eye's location. Xanathar's lair beneath Skullport. The party needs to determine how to enter, how to navigate the surveillance network, where the Eye is held inside, and where Xanathar will be during the operation.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:61: **Gladiatorial Transport (via Terasse Estate).** Discovered through the Terasse Estate mini-heist in **Faction Outposts**. The trap door at Terasse leads to an underground dock where guests are blindfolded and transported by boat to Area X4, arriving as spectators during the gladiatorial games. Advantage: legitimate spectator cover. The crowd and noise provide concealment; DC checks to remain unnoticed in spectator areas drop by 3 during active fights. The party can use tournament sessions to observe NPC movement patterns and confirm Xanathar's location. Disadvantage: the party arrives unarmed. Weapons and obviously magical items are confiscated at X4. The party must smuggle equipment (DC 16 Dexterity (Sleight of Hand) per item) or recover it from Area X9, the equipment cache. If the party's escalation tier is Alert or higher, the tournament may be suspended — this entry method loses its crowd cover and the party arrives into a searched, guarded X4.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:75: *Xanathar's Location.* Xanathar is in X19 (the Sanctum) with Sylgar. It has barely left for weeks. The Eye is there. So is Xanathar.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:77: This means the party cannot simply walk into X19 and take the Eye — not unless they create a window. The dream nullifier in X20 exists for this purpose: destroying it sends Xanathar into the corridor to find Ahmaergo, giving the party approximately 4 minutes. A party that found X20 through reconnaissance or Thorvin's guidance has a clean extraction opportunity. A party that didn't must extract the Eye while a paranoid beholder is in the room.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:95: If the gazer is destroyed (13 HP, AC 13), Thorvin speaks openly for the first time. His relief is immediate and genuine. He confirms what the party may have begun to suspect: Nihiloor's domain (X23–X27) contains evidence that would change everything the party thinks they know about who has been running Xanathar's Guild.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:99: *Inside Ally: Nar'l Xibrindas (X35).* The only drow in the lair, which Xanathar considers proof of its own cosmopolitan generosity. Nar'l's grell bodyguard carries a kill-on-disloyalty order from Xanathar, an instruction the grell will carry out without hesitation. Nar'l cannot dismiss it. He can only avoid triggering it.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:103: With BD allegiance revealed: Nar'l reassesses, then cooperates warily. He can suppress the alarm signal for a specific area for up to ten minutes (logged as a maintenance calibration), redirect a guard patrol once per session, and confirm Xanathar's current location. He will not act against the grell. He will sacrifice the party if doing so is the only way to preserve his cover.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:105: If a party member is BD: Nar'l recognizes them as colleagues and cooperates fully — no hesitation, no wary reassessment. He shares the complete guard rotation, confirms Xanathar's location in X19 and the dream nullifier's purpose, and actively manages alarms rather than offering a single suppression. He also reveals the existence of X14 (the secret passage to X19) if the party has not already found it. The grell constraint still applies: he will not act against it regardless of orders, because the grell is Xanathar's mechanism, not Jarlaxle's. He will evacuate immediately if the smokepowder option is chosen. One additional consequence of BD membership: the Jarlaxle simultaneous heist does not fire. Jarlaxle knew the party was BD, trusted them to handle it, and is waiting for Nar'l's report rather than sending a competing team.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:111: Consequences: Xanathar is destroyed if present in the lair, or escapes through the Undermountain connection at X4 if the GM wants to preserve the thread. The Guild collapses into factional fighting between Ahmaergo and surviving lieutenants. Nar'l and Thorvin must be evacuated or they die. Thorvin will not leave the lair's surviving workers; Nar'l will leave without hesitation. Nihiloor was not in his domain at the time and escapes to Undermountain Level 17 with his three projects intact.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:114: *The Dream Nullifier (X20).* A copper bowl the size of a serving dish, mounted in a maintenance alcove between X19 and X21. A Passive Perception score of 15 or higher notices it while passing through the corridor. A DC 13 Intelligence (Arcana) check identifies its function: it prevents Xanathar from dreaming of other beholders, waking it before the dream takes hold.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:116: Destroying it (antimagic field or dispel magic against DC 15) triggers behavioral change within 3 minutes: intrusive dream-images of other beholders crowd Xanathar's perception. It leaves X19 to find Ahmaergo, demanding explanation, seeking confirmation that the lair is still secure. It moves toward X18 or X12. X19 is empty. The party has approximately 4 minutes.
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:123: | Suspicious | X1 unwatched; X4 checked | DC checks +2 | Extra pairs at X2, X4 | Runs; Xanathar remains in X19 |
campaign/structure/arc-f-xanathars-lair.md:147: **X7 — Cell Block.** Eight cells cut into rock, three occupied.
